Prior work addresses it either through pixel-level prediction or difference captioning, neither of which is sufficient to reliably measure how well models detect and describe such changes. We introduce SPOT-THE-SHIFT, a human-verified benchmark for grounded image difference captioning of long-term changes in real-world driving scenes. Our benchmark provides natural language captions and spatial masks for structural changes across each image pair. We further propose an evaluation protocol that reliably assesses models' captioning ability, validated through human studies. Benchmarking state-of-the-art MLLMs, we find that models struggle with the fine-grained multi-image spatial capability required for this task. Finally, we develop a synthetic data generation pipeline that improves an off-the-shelf MLLM without sacrificing general capabilities.

</details>


### [67] [SceneHI: High-Resolution 3D-Consistent Scene Texturing with Controllable Illumination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10363)
*Athanasios Tragakis,Marco Aversa,Daniela Ivanova,Chaitanya Kaul,Roderick Murray-Smith,Daniele Faccio,Paul Henderson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SceneHI 是首个将高分辨率、光照感知先验从2D扩散模型迁移到3D纹理合成的框架，无需微调或优化即可直接在3D对象上生成高质量纹理。它结合了3D一致性、高分辨率保真度和物理合理的烘焙阴影，通过精确的像素到纹素映射确保多视角一致性，并利用高分辨率潜在纹理（HRLTs）作为持续的画布进行渐进去噪，同时通过光照感知生成步骤将真实几何一致的阴影嵌入纹理图集，显著提升视觉质量并减少80%生成时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在3D纹理合成中难以同时实现高分辨率、多视角一致性与物理真实的阴影效果，且通常需要复杂微调或优化。本文旨在突破2D扩散模型在3D应用中的局限，直接将高分辨率纹理生成能力迁移至3D场景，以满足复杂多物体环境下的生产级需求。

Method: 提出基于精确像素到纹素映射的多视角对齐机制，采用高分辨率潜在纹理（HRLTs）作为共享基础纹理，通过相机视图在潜在像素空间中逐步去噪；引入光照感知生成阶段，将几何一致的阴影直接嵌入纹理图集，实现端到端的3D纹理合成。

Result: SceneHI 实现了高视觉保真度的3D纹理生成，相比现有场景级方法生成时间减少80%，并在复杂多物体环境中保持了强3D一致性和物理合理性。

Conclusion: SceneHI 成功将2D扩散模型的高分辨率纹理生成能力扩展至3D领域，实现了无需微调的高效、高质量3D纹理合成，为真实感场景生成提供了可直接应用于生产的工作流。

Abstract: SceneHI is a framework that lifts high-resolution, illumination-aware priors from 2D diffusion models to perform 3D texture synthesis. It is the first to demonstrate that high-resolution textures, previously limited to 2D synthesis, can be generated directly on 3D objects without model fine-tuning or optimization. Designed for complex, multi-object environments, SceneHI uniquely combines 3D-consistency, high-resolution fidelity, and physically plausible baked shadows within a single generative pipeline. To enforce strict geometric coherence, we introduce an exact analytical pixel-to-texel mapping that aligns diffusion trajectories across multiple viewpoints. We utilize High-Resolution Latent Textures (HRLTs) as a persistent canvas for gradually denoised textures, while camera views perform the denoising steps in latent pixel space. This ensures a shared base texture that can be subsequently refined to high resolution without compromising multi-view consistency. Finally, a light-aware generative pass embeds realistic geometry-consistent shadows directly into the atlases, bridging the gap to production workflows. SceneHI achieves high visual fidelity while reducing generation time by 80% compared to existing scene-level methods.

</details>


### [68] [Beyond Weak Labels: Prompt-Guided Local Refinement for Weakly Supervised Water Segmentation in High-Resolution Multispectral Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10371)
*Muhammad Farhan Humayun,Mohammad Imangholiloo,Afifah Shah,Tomi Westerlund,Jukka Heikkonen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种两阶段弱监督水体分割框架，利用官方水文矢量数据生成伪标签，并通过结构化组件提示进行局部优化，显著提升了水体分割精度，尤其改善了岸线清晰度和细小水体的识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率水体映射对环境监测至关重要，但像素级标注成本高昂；虽然官方水文矢量可提供弱监督信号，但存在边界噪声、时间不匹配及小水体遗漏等问题，因此需要一种有效方法来提升基于伪标签的水体分割性能。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：第一阶段基于栅格化矢量伪标签学习初始掩码；第二阶段将这些掩码转换为结构化组件提示，用于局部精细化调整，从而修正局部误差。

Result: 在人工校正的验证集上，该方法使SegFormer-B0的IoU从0.9509提升至0.9535，U-Net从0.9408提升至0.9486；F1分数也相应提高，显著改善了岸线清晰度、减少了边界溢出，并增强了对细小水体的分割能力。

Conclusion: 提示引导的局部精修能有效弥补全局训练监督的不足，显著提升基于伪标签的水体分割质量，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: High-resolution water mapping supports environmental monitoring and related applications, but accurate pixel-level labels are difficult and costly to produce. Official hydrographic vectors provide scalable weak supervision, but they contain artifacts like boundary noise, temporal mismatch, and omissions of small water structures. We propose a two-stage framework for weakly supervised water segmentation in high resolution multispectral imagery. Stage 1 learns initial masks from rasterized vector pseudo-labels, and Stage 2 converts these masks into structured component-wise prompts for localized refinement. On a manually corrected validation set, refinement improves SegFormer-B0 from 0.9509 to 0.9535 IoU and U-Net from 0.9408 to 0.9486 IoU, with corresponding F1 gains from 0.9749 to 0.9762 and 0.9695 to 0.9736. It leads to sharper shorelines, reduced boundary spillover, and better thin-structure delineation. The results indicate that prompt-guided refinement can improve pseudo-label-based water segmentation by targeting local errors that are poorly captured by global training supervision.

</details>


### [69] [PACE: Perceived-Latency-Aware Cascading Service Routing and Filler Control for QoE-Efficient Retrieval-Augmented Dialogue Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10372)
*Lin Huang,Yujuan Tan,Weisheng Li,Lixiang Zeng,Kun Yang,Suihan Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PACE 是一个用于检索增强型对话服务的框架，将感知响应时间（PTFR）作为QoE目标，并在质量/成本约束下最小化该指标。它联合控制响应来源的选择和等待期间的内容填充，通过负载自适应级联路由、路径填充控制器和波动性感知缓存准入机制实现。在7.5万次CarQA请求上，级联将P95的纯LLM PTFR减半（0.29秒对比0.53秒），自适应控制器在高负载下以同等质量性能优于RAG 2.4倍（P95为0.41秒），填充控制器减少94%调用且无冲突，波动性感知缓存将过时答案从86%降至0%。门控规则确保控制器性能不低于基线，暴露时间不超过一个持有周期。这是首次对部署服务中填充-答案冲突风险进行量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在级联路由、语义缓存或自适应检索方面存在局限，无法同时优化响应速度与资源使用效率；尤其缺乏对等待期间内容填充与最终答案之间潜在冲突的有效管理，导致用户体验下降。因此需要一种能联合控制响应源选择与等待填充内容的新框架，以提升感知响应时间并保证服务质量。

Method: PACE框架包含三个核心组件：负载自适应级联路由，根据系统负载动态选择检索路径；联合路径填充控制器，协调填充内容与最终答案的一致性；以及波动性感知缓存准入策略，避免缓存过时数据。此外引入门控规则，确保性能不劣于基线，同时限制暴露风险。

Result: 在75,000次CarQA请求测试中，级联机制使纯大模型的P95 PTFR从0.53秒降低至0.29秒；自适应控制器在高负载下达到0.41秒的P95，性能优于RAG 2.4倍，且质量相当；路径填充控制器减少94%的外部调用，无冲突发生；波动性感知缓存将过时回答率从86%降至0%；门控机制有效保障性能不低于基线，暴露时间受控。

Conclusion: PACE是首个在实际部署中量化并解决填充-答案冲突风险的框架，通过联合优化响应源选择与等待填充内容，在保证服务质量的前提下显著降低感知响应时间，适用于高并发、低延迟要求的对话系统场景。

Abstract: We present the PACE, a framework for retrieval-augmented dialogue serving that formalizes Perceived Time-to-First-Response (PTFR) as a QoE objective and minimizes it under quality/cost constraints. Unlike prior work on cascaded routing, semantic caching, or adaptive retrieval, PACE jointly controls which answer source composes the response and what fills the waiting window. Deployed on a humanoid-robot sales service, it combines three mechanisms: a load-adaptive cascading router, a joint path-filler controller, and volatility-aware cache admission. On 75k CarQA requests, the cascade halves pure-LLM PTFR at P95 (0.29 vs 0.53s at c16). The adaptive controller reaches 0.41s P95, outperforming RAG by 2.4 times at high load with equal quality. The filler controller cuts calls by 94% with zero conflict. Volatility-aware admission reduces stale answers from 86% to 0%. A gating rule ensures the controller never worse than the baseline, with exposure bounded by one hold period. This is the first quantification of filler-answer conflict risk in deployed services.

</details>


### [70] [Shape-guided Gaussian Splatting for Sparse-View X-ray 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10376)
*Pranav Poudel,Florence Dell'Aniello Picard,Nairouz Shehata,Frédéric Lavoie,Herve Lombaert*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于形状引导的高斯点阵框架，用于稀疏视角X射线3D重建。通过将高斯点的位置引导至解剖学上有效的配置，并结合基于图谱的密度正则化，显著提升了在极少视角（如5视图）下的重建质量。相比现有最优的高斯点阵方法，本方法在PSNR上提升2.83 dB，实现了更符合解剖结构的重建结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏视角下的X射线3D重建面临严重不适定问题，传统高斯点阵方法依赖大量投影视图，视角过少时重建质量急剧下降，因缺乏解剖学先验信息导致优化自由度过高。而人体解剖结构具有相似的几何与密度特征，其变化范围有限，可通过统计形貌模型捕捉，因此引入解剖学约束可提升重建精度与一致性。

Method: 提出形状引导的高斯点阵框架，通过将高斯点位置约束于解剖学有效空间，并引入基于图谱的密度正则化项，使优化过程受解剖先验引导，从而在极少数视角下仍能实现高质量、解剖一致的重建。

Result: 在仅5个投影视角的情况下，相比当前最先进的高斯点阵基线方法，本方法在PSNR上提升2.83 dB，显著改善了稀疏视图下的重建质量，并确保了重建结果的解剖合理性。

Conclusion: 本研究验证了引入解剖学先验对稀疏视角X射线3D重建的关键作用。所提出的形状引导高斯点阵方法在极低视角数下仍能生成高保真、解剖一致的体积重建，为降低辐射剂量同时保持图像质量提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Sparse-view X-ray 3D reconstruction is essential for reducing radiation exposure, but recovering a density field from a handful of X-ray projections is severely ill-posed. Recently, 3D Gaussian Splatting has achieved state-of-the-art performance in sparse-view reconstruction by representing the volume using explicit, optimized primitives, but it requires dozens of projected views. With fewer views, reconstruction quality degrades severely since the explicit primitives are optimized freely without any anatomical information. Anatomical structures, in contrast, share similar geometry and density across a population. Their variations are bounded within a limited range that statistical shape models can capture. This paper proposes a shape-guided Gaussian splatting framework for sparse-view X-ray 3D reconstructions. Our contribution lies in driving Gaussian positions toward anatomically valid configurations, alongside atlas-based density regularization. Our method ensures anatomically consistent reconstruction and improves PSNR by 2.83 dB over a state-of-the-art Gaussian splatting baseline with as few as 5 views. Code Available: https://github.com/polyshape-lab/ShapeGuidedGaussian

</details>


### [71] [Enhanced Deformable Convolution with Center-invariant Offset and Edge-aware Mask](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10387)
*Yixiao Li,Xiaoyuan Yang,Jin Jiang,Minghao Zou,Guanghui Yue,Baoquan Zhao,Jun Liu,Wei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出增强可变形卷积网络（EDCN）用于语义分割，通过引入新型增强可变形卷积（EDC），结合中心不变偏移模块（COM）和边缘感知掩码模块（EMM），提升特征表示的精确性与空间适应能力。COM利用大卷积核并消除中心点形变，获取更丰富的空间信息；EMM基于Sobel边缘检测判断内容重要性，选择性地应用形变，减少无关区域干扰。实验表明EDC在主流分割数据集上优于现有可变形卷积方法，消融实验验证各组件有效性，可视化展示其增强的空间适应性和目标聚焦能力。此外，还分析了其在更大卷积核下的可扩展性。代码将公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可变形卷积网络在语义分割任务中受限于密集形变偏移和缺乏长程依赖关系，难以实现精准且合理的特征形变建模，因此需要一种更高效、更具针对性的可变形机制。

Method: 提出增强可变形卷积（EDC），包含中心不变偏移模块（COM）和边缘感知掩码模块（EMM）。COM使用大卷积核并抑制中心点形变，以获得更符合目标的偏移；EMM通过Sobel边缘检测识别图像内容重要性，动态控制形变应用范围，避免对低信息区域进行无效形变。

Result: EDC在多个主流语义分割数据集上超越Deformable ConvNets V1-V4及完整可变形卷积等先进方法，在不同解码器设置下表现优异；消融实验验证各模块有效性；可视化显示其具备更强的空间适应能力和目标聚焦能力；扩展实验表明其在图像分类任务中适用于更大卷积核。

Conclusion: EDCN通过引入EDC显著提升了可变形卷积在语义分割中的性能，有效解决了传统方法因密集偏移和忽略内容重要性导致的形变不准确问题，具有良好的可扩展性与实用性，代码将开源。

Abstract: Deformable convolution networks have recently become popular for many computer vision tasks, especially for semantic segmentation, because of their exceptional capabilities in dynamic spatial modeling. However, due to the dense deformable offsets and the lack of longer-range dependencies, they can not fully adopt proper and precise deformations for feature representations. To tackle the issues, in this paper, we propose Enhanced Deformable ConvNets (EDCN) for semantic segmentation. Specifically, a novel Enhanced Deformable Convolution (EDC) is exploited in the decoder, which integrates the Center-invariant Offset Module (COM) and Edge-aware Mask Module (EMM). The COM employs larger kernels and eliminates deformations at the kernel center, obtaining offsets that are more in line with the target from richer spatial information. Concurrently, the EMM obtains the significance of image content via Sobel edge detection, then selectively applies deformations based on the content significance, minimizing unnecessary deformations associated with relatively less important information, thereby avoiding impact from less informative regions. Experiments show that EDC outperforms state-of-the-art deformable convolution variants, including Deformable ConvNets V1-V4 and Entire Deformable ConvNets, across mainstream segmentation datasets with various decoder settings. Moreover, ablation studies confirm the effectiveness of each component. In addition, visualizations illustrate that EDC enhances spatial adaptation and target focus. We further analyze the extendibility of EDC to larger kernels on the image classification benchmark. Code will be publicly released.

</details>


### [72] [Advanced Brain Tissue Imaging with Data-Consistent Diffusion Priors in Laminographic X-Ray Nanoimaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10456)
*Wenxuan Fang,Abraham L. Levitan,Ana Diaz,Carles Bosch,Adrian Wanner,Andreas T. Schaefer,Mirko Holler,Tomas Aidukas,Nicholas W. Phillips,Yuxin Zhang,Alexandra Pacureanu,Manuel Guizar-Sicairos,Luis Barba*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LUCID 是一种结合多视角扩散先验与投影域数据一致性的新框架，用于解决纳米级脑成像中因倾斜采集导致的傅里叶空间信息缺失问题。它在模拟和真实实验数据上均表现出色，有效恢复了缺失的傅里叶成分，并提升了空间保真度，且无需依赖大量标注数据即可泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统层析成像方法在倾斜采集下存在傅里叶空间覆盖不全的问题，导致图像中出现缺失锥区域，引发结构失真。直接使用3D深度学习受限于数据稀缺和计算成本，因此需要一种既能利用结构先验又可保持数据一致性的高效重建方法。

Method: LUCID 采用多视图扩散模型作为3D结构先验，结合投影域的数据一致性约束，将扩散先验与层析成像的正向模型严格对齐，实现对缺失傅里叶成分的有效重建。

Result: 在模拟和真实层析数据上，LUCID 显著提升了空间分辨率和结构保真度，成功恢复了缺失的傅里叶信息，且在仅用完全采样断层数据训练的情况下仍具备良好泛化能力。

Conclusion: LUCID 提供了一种高效、鲁棒的层析成像重建方案，为高通量纳米级脑连接组学提供了关键技术支撑。

Abstract: Nanoscale imaging of mammalian brains is critical for connectomics. X-ray laminography enables high-throughput imaging of extended, plate-like biological specimens. However, the tilted acquisition geometry leads to incomplete Fourier-space coverage, giving rise to a missing-cone of information. Conventional reconstruction methods cannot recover unmeasured information within the cone, resulting in artifacts that distort fine brain structures. While resolving these requires modeling 3D structure, direct 3D deep learning approaches are limited by data scarcity and computational cost. Here we introduce LUCID (Laminography with Unified Consistent Diffusion), a framework that combines multi-view diffusion priors with projection-domain data consistency. LUCID integrates complementary 3D structural information while enforcing strict alignment with the laminography forward model. On simulated datasets, LUCID substantially improves spatial fidelity and restores missing Fourier components, outperforming baseline methods. Applied to experimental laminography data, LUCID generalizes robustly despite being trained exclusively on fully sampled tomographic volumes, and effectively recovers unmeasured Fourier information.

</details>


### [73] [AgroVisNet: A lightweight Convolutional Network and the BD-PlantDX Expert-Validated Benchmark for Radish, Potato and Pointed Gourd Disease Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10469)
*Md. Abdullah Mandal,Saad Ahmed,Md. Khalid Syfullah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AgroVisNet轻量级模型与BD-PlantDX专家验证数据集，针对孟加拉国萝卜、马铃薯和尖瓣瓜的病害诊断，实现99.52%高精度，在低功耗设备上仅需0.46MB存储与8.40ms推理时间，且具备良好泛化性与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有植物病害诊断系统受限于非本地作物数据、缺乏领域专家验证及模型参数过大难以部署于低成本硬件，限制了其在农业资源匮乏地区的实际应用价值。

Method: 设计AgroVisNet模型，结合分组瓶颈残差块（含通道与空间注意力）、多尺度深度可分离块及双池化分类头；构建并由专家验证的BD-PlantDX数据集包含12,432张田间图像，覆盖12类健康与病态样本；采用端到端训练策略，并进行量化部署与多轮消融实验。

Result: 在BD-PlantDX上达到99.52%测试准确率与99.52%加权F1，优于六个ImageNet预训练轻量级骨干网络，参数减少8.7至16.8倍，计算量降低1.3至8.5倍；量化后模型仅0.46MB，推理速度8.40ms/图像，跨种子与跨数据集表现稳定，Grad-CAM显示预测聚焦于病斑区域。

Conclusion: AgroVisNet结合高质量专家标注数据与高效网络结构，实现了高精度、低资源消耗的移动端植物病害诊断，具备良好的实用性、鲁棒性与可解释性，适用于偏远地区农业场景。

Abstract: Automated plant disease diagnosis is increasingly deployed on farmer-held devices in regions where agronomic expertise is scarce and network connectivity is unreliable. Three obstacles limit its practical value: public benchmarks are dominated by a small set of non-native crops, region-specific datasets are rarely validated by domain experts, and the architectures that reach competitive accuracy carry parameter budgets that are unsuited to low-cost hardware. We propose AgroVisNet, a compact convolutional network trained from scratch, together with BD-PlantDX, an expert-validated benchmark of 12,432 field images spanning 12 classes of radish, potato and pointed gourd in healthy and diseased states, collected across the Bogura and Nilphamari districts of Bangladesh. AgroVisNet couples grouped bottleneck residual blocks carrying sequential channel and spatial attention with multi-scale depthwise blocks and a dual-pooling classification head, reaching 290,572 trainable parameters. On BD-PlantDX the model attains 99.52% test accuracy and 99.52% weighted F1, exceeding all six ImageNet-pretrained lightweight backbones evaluated under an identical protocol while using 8.7 to 16.8 times fewer parameters and 1.3 to 8.5 times fewer multiply-accumulate operations. Exported for deployment, the model quantises to a 0.46 MB full-integer network at a 0.22 percentage-point accuracy cost and classifies an image in 8.40 ms on a single CPU. Across five random seeds accuracy remains at 99.57 +- 0.10%, a ten-variant ablation isolates the contribution of each component, and the same architecture transfers without redesign to two independently collected datasets at 98.71% and 99.05% accuracy. Grad-CAM evidence indicates that predictions rest on lesion-bearing leaf regions rather than on background cues.

</details>


### [74] [Artificial Intelligence Literacy and Sustainable Development: An Ethical Governance and Development Goals Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10489)
*Md. Masudul Islam,Mirza Niaz Morshed,Md. Shafiqul Islam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究将人工智能素养定位为一种治理能力，提出六级人工智能推理与伦理分类体系，强调伦理判断与战略远见的重要性，并通过300名跨领域参与者调查发现公众在技术认知上较强，但在伦理与治理准备度上存在明显短板。研究建议将基于素养的能力融入教育、政策与治理机制，以推动可持续发展目标的负责任实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前人工智能发展面临伦理与治理挑战，仅具备基础素养不足以支持可持续发展，需提升公众在伦理判断和战略决策方面的能力，以实现对AI的负责任管理。

Method: 构建六级人工智能推理与伦理分类体系，结合问卷调查（n=300）分析不同专业背景人群的AI素养水平，识别关键预测因子，并提出整合教育、治理与可持续发展的框架。

Result: 技术意识较强但伦理与治理准备不足；伦理推理与反思性思维是实现可信、可持续人工智能使用的最强预测因素。

Conclusion: 应将AI素养相关能力嵌入教育体系、机构政策与治理机制，以促进公平、负责任地推进可持续发展目标。

Abstract: AI literacy provides foundational competencies that support ethical, transparent, and sustainable technological development, although higher-order capabilities such as governance, critical evaluation, and strategic decision-making extend beyond basic literacy into advanced levels of AI competency. This study positions AI literacy as a governance capacity that complements and strengthens all 17 SDGs. It introduces a six-level taxonomy of artificial intelligence reasoning and ethics that extends traditional learning models by incorporating ethical judgement and strategic foresight. This taxonomy forms the foundation of an integrated framework linking education, governance, and sustainable development. A survey of 300 participants from diverse professional backgrounds within a national context which reveals strong technical awareness but limited ethical and governance readiness, highlighting critical gaps in public capacity to manage artificial intelligence responsibly. Findings show that ethical reasoning and reflective thinking are the strongest predictors of sustainable and trustworthy artificial intelligence use. The study proposed to embed literacy-based competencies into curricula, institutional policies, and governance mechanisms to accelerate equitable and responsible progress toward sustainable development goals

</details>


### [75] [Cross-Model Agreement as a Deployment-Time Reliability Signal for Automatic Polyp Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10495)
*Siddharth Gupta,Jitin Singla*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需参考的基于裁判的质量评估框架RBQE，通过主分割模型与独立训练的裁判模型在相同图像上的一致性来衡量可靠性。在1,223张外部测试图像上评估，跨架构裁判（如SegFormer-B0）表现最佳（ROC-AUC=0.960），显著优于同架构裁判和UNet++，且超过测试时增强基线0.055。即使排除空掩码情况后，RBQE仍保持优势。该方法能提升保留预测的平均Dice值，支持选择性预测，仅需一次额外推理，具有实用性与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实时结肠镜检查中，推理阶段缺乏真实标签，导致息肉分割模型可能无声失败。需要一种无需真实标签的可靠性评估方法，以识别不可靠预测并提升系统安全性。

Method: 提出参考自由的裁判质量评估（RBQE）框架，利用主分割模型与独立训练的裁判模型在相同图像上的预测一致性（使用同意Dice指标）作为可靠性信号。采用多种裁判配置（同架构、跨架构、不同初始化、提示耦合等）进行对比实验。

Result: 跨架构裁判（如SegFormer-B0）表现最优（ROC-AUC=0.960），显著优于同架构裁判和基线方法；即使在排除空掩码的情况下，其性能优势仍扩大；随着低一致性样本被剔除，保留样本的平均Dice值上升，验证了选择性预测的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于跨模型一致性的质量评估是自动化息肉分割中一种实用、可解释的可靠性框架，仅需一次额外前向传播，适用于临床实时应用。

Abstract: In real-time colonoscopy, ground-truth annotations are unavailable at inference, so polyp segmentation models can fail silently. We propose Referee-Based Quality Estimation (RBQE), a reference-free framework measuring agreement between a primary segmentation model and an independently trained referee on the same image. RBQE is evaluated on a standardized 1,223-image external benchmark drawn from four public datasets, using four referee configurations chosen to separate two design axes: referee independence and architectural diversity. Using a common Agreement Dice descriptor, a same-architecture referee differing from the primary model only in random initialization already yields a useful reliability signal (ROC-AUC = 0.923), showing that independent training alone is sufficient. Cross-architecture referees improve further: SegFormer-B0 achieves the strongest performance (ROC-AUC = 0.960), significantly outperforming the same-architecture control and UNet++, and exceeding a representative Test-Time Augmentation baseline by 0.055 ROC-AUC under an identical protocol, whereas a prompt-coupled MedSAM referee underperforms despite maximal architectural diversity. Because empty-mask agreement is trivially separable, we also report a restricted evaluation excluding such cases: ROC-AUC falls to 0.876 (SegFormer-B0, 1,046 images) and 0.783 (same-architecture control, 975 images), yet RBQE's margin over both baselines widens on this identical subset. RBQE additionally increases the mean Dice of retained predictions as low-agreement cases are progressively rejected, supporting selective prediction, and requires only one additional deterministic referee forward pass at inference. Our study therefore supports cross-model agreement as a practical, interpretable reliability framework for automated polyp segmentation.

</details>


### [76] [Field Converter: Geometry-Initialized Temporal Residual Refinement for World-Grounded Player Pose Estimation from Soccer Broadcasts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10498)
*Simon Khan,Laurent Gajny,Jennyfer Lecompte,Sébastien Laporte*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Field Converter框架，利用相机与球场几何信息初始化球员根节点位置，通过时间残差修正实现世界坐标系下的3D人体姿态估计。在非匹配序列上，残差优化将根节点误差从49cm降至10cm，最终世界空间MPJPE达13.2cm，证明了残差预测优于直接回归，且时间上下文比具体时序模型结构更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单目体育广播中恢复球员在共享度量世界坐标系中的3D姿态仍具挑战，尤其需精确定位根节点而非仅相对身体重建。现有方法受限于几何初始化不准确及缺乏有效的时间一致性建模。

Method: 首先利用相机与球场几何进行射线-地面交点初始化根节点；随后设计基于图像、姿态、相机与几何线索的时序残差修正模块，采用MLP、TCN或Transformer实现帧间一致性优化。

Result: 在匹配分离的评估序列上，残差修正使根节点误差由几何初始化的49cm降至14cm（MLP）、10cm（TCN），Transformer达到11cm；最终世界空间MPJPE为13.2cm，显著优于直接回归方法。

Conclusion: 基于几何初始化的时序残差框架能有效提升单目体育视频中世界坐标系下3D球员姿态估计精度，时间上下文对性能至关重要，而空中运动仍是主要瓶颈。

Abstract: Recovering 3D human pose from monocular sports broadcasts remains challenging when players must be localized in a shared metric world coordinate system rather than only reconstructed relative to their own body. We introduce Field Converter, a geometry-initialized temporal residual framework for world-grounded 3D player pose estimation from calibrated soccer broadcasts. Our method first uses camera and pitch geometry to initialize the player root through ray-ground intersection, then predicts a temporal residual correction from pose, image, camera, and geometric cues. On match-disjoint evaluation sequences, residual refinement reduces root error from 49cm with geometry alone to 14cm with a frame-wise MLP and 10cm with a TCN, while a Transformer achieves a comparable 11cm. The resulting world-space MPJPE reaches 13.2cm, and ablations show that residual prediction clearly outperforms direct global-root regression while temporal context matters more than the specific temporal backbone. Failure analysis further identifies airborne motion as the main limitation of the ground-based geometric initialization.

</details>


### [77] [BrainTaskonomy: Learning How to Pretrain and What to Transfer in fMRI Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10518)
*Junfeng Xia,Wenhao Ye,Junxiang Zhang,Jiayu Zuo,Mo Wang,Quanying Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究如何通过测量的学习关系组织fMRI预训练和微调阶段，而不修改骨干网络。预训练阶段使用轻量级Brain-DiT代理估计十个fMRI域之间的难度与定向促进关系，生成优先级引导的累积域课程，并结合高到低噪声的时间步调度与联合巩固策略。微调阶段通过控制一阶和高阶迁移构建有向任务拓扑，利用预算整数规划（BIP）选择直接监督的源任务和目标特定路径。实验表明，联合优先域课程与高低噪声时间步调度使v-NMSE、PSD-NMSE、FC-MSE分别降低6.5%、16.3%、10.5%，相比均匀采样显著提升性能，并在六项域内与域外任务上表现优异。任务拓扑揭示了不对称、目标依赖的迁移特性，封闭测试显示当高阶路径空间可用时，BIP策略描述增益显著高于随机对照组。结果支持基于学习关系而非独立扁平集合来组织fMRI预训练与微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前fMRI基础模型虽整合异质数据，但预训练域与下游任务常被视为独立扁平集合，缺乏对学习关系的建模，限制了跨域与跨任务的高效迁移。本文旨在探索是否可通过测量的学习关系，统一优化预训练与微调阶段，提升模型泛化与效率。

Method: 提出轻量级Brain-DiT代理用于估计十域间学习难度与定向促进关系；构建优先级引导的累积域课程与高到低噪声时间步调度；在微调阶段建立有向任务拓扑，利用预算整数规划（BIP）选择最优源任务与目标特定迁移路径。

Result: 联合优先域课程与高低噪声时间步调度使v-NMSE、PSD-NMSE、FC-MSE分别下降6.5%、16.3%、10.5%；在六项域内/外任务中表现优异；任务拓扑揭示非对称、目标依赖的迁移模式；封闭测试中，高阶路径空间下BIP策略优于随机对照组，说明其更强的描述能力。

Conclusion: 应基于测量的学习关系组织fMRI预训练与微调过程，而非将域与任务视为独立扁平集合。该方法显著提升模型性能与迁移效率，为构建更智能、自适应的fMRI基础模型提供新范式。

Abstract: fMRI foundation models increasingly aggregate heterogeneous data across brain states, cohorts, and acquisition settings, yet pretraining domains are commonly treated as a flat mixture and downstream tasks are adapted independently. We study whether measured learning relations can organize both stages without modifying the backbone. During pretraining, a lightweight Brain-DiT proxy estimates difficulty and directed facilitation across ten fMRI domains, yielding a priority-guided cumulative domain curriculum combined with high-to-low-noise timestep scheduling and joint consolidation. During adaptation, controlled first- and higher-order transfer across fifteen tasks constructs a directed taskonomy, from which budgeted integer programming (BIP) selects directly supervised source tasks and target-specific routes. The joint priority-domain and high-to-low-timestep curriculum reduces v-NMSE, PSD-NMSE, and FC-MSE by 6.5%, 16.3%, and 10.5%, respectively, relative to uniform sampling over both dimensions, and shows strong downstream performance across six in- and out-of-domain tasks. The taskonomy reveals asymmetric, target-dependent transfer, while exploratory sealed-test evaluation shows larger descriptive gains for BIP policies when higher-order route spaces are available than for matched random controls. Together, these findings support organizing fMRI pretraining and adaptation by measured learning relations rather than treating domains and tasks as independent flat sets.

</details>


### [78] [Precision in Rice Variety Classification using Stacking-Based Ensemble Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10524)
*Md. Masudul Islam,Galib Muhammad Shahriar Himel,Md. Golam Moazzam,Mohammad Shorif Uddin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于视觉特征的稻米品种识别框架，通过构建包含20个品种的综合数据集并采用堆叠集成模型，实现了100%的分类准确率，并开发了配套移动应用，使非专业用户也能通过手机拍照轻松识别稻米品种。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稻米作为全球重要主食，品种繁多且外部特征差异显著，现有方法难以实现精准分类，导致品种混杂等欺诈行为频发，亟需高效、可靠的识别技术以保障供应链质量与透明度。

Method: 构建了一个包含20个稻米品种的视觉数据集，采用堆叠集成学习模型进行分类，并将模型部署至移动端应用，支持智能手机拍摄图像实时识别。

Result: 所提方法在20个稻米品种上实现了100%的分类准确率，且移动应用可被普通用户便捷使用，显著提升识别效率与准确性。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了先进机器学习技术在防止稻米欺诈和保障品质控制方面的巨大潜力，为农业领域自动化作物识别和精准农业的发展提供了有力支持。

Abstract: Rice, a staple food for a significant portion of the global population, exhibits remarkable diversity in its varieties, presenting substantial challenges for accurate identification by consumers, traders, and farmers. This complexity often facilitates fraudulent practices, such as the unauthorized mixing of rice types, which undermines quality and trust in the supply chain. Despite its critical importance, existing research falls short of providing robust and efficient methods for precise rice variety classification based on external characteristics like color, size, and texture. To address this gap, our study introduces a comprehensive rice variety identification framework designed to enhance transparency and quality assurance. We developed a stacked ensemble model tailored for rice variety classification and curated a comprehensive dataset comprising 20 rice varieties, each distinguished by unique visual attributes. The proposed approach achieved an unprecedented classification accuracy of 100%. Furthermore, we integrated our model into a mobile application, enabling even novice users to effortlessly identify rice varieties using grain images from a smartphone camera. These findings underscore the transformative potential of advanced machine learning techniques in mitigating fraudulent practices and ensuring stringent rice quality control. Our work holds significant implications for agricultural stakeholders, paving the way for automated crop identification systems and advancing precision agriculture practices.

</details>


### [79] [Guiding Image-to-3D Generation with Test-Time Partial Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10531)
*Jerred Chen,Simon Weber,Ronald Clark*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练的框架，通过测试时引导方式将部分几何观测信息融入预训练的图像到3D生成模型中，利用射线一致的观测似然函数结合表面占据和自由空间证据，显著提升几何保真度与视觉质量，且无需微调或重新训练模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像到3D生成模型虽能生成视觉逼真的3D资产，但其几何结构往往受输入图像约束较弱，难以满足高几何保真度的应用需求；而实际场景中常存在部分几何观测信息，因此需要一种不依赖重训练的方法来融合这些观测以改进生成结果。

Method: 提出基于射线一致的观测似然函数，作用于模型的占据表示，同时利用表面占据和自由空间的证据进行测试时引导，实现对预训练模型的无修改优化。

Result: 在SAM 3D及其多视角扩展上的实验表明，该方法在不同可观测程度下均显著提升了几何保真度和视觉质量，验证了其有效性与通用性。

Conclusion: 预训练的图像到3D模型可通过显式的测试时引导有效整合部分几何观测信息，增强生成结果的几何准确性，同时保持原有生成先验，无需修改底层模型。

Abstract: Image-to-3D models can generate visually compelling 3D assets from a single RGB image, but their geometry is often only loosely constrained by the available observations, limiting their use in applications that require geometric fidelity. In many real-world settings, however, partial geometric observations of the object may be available at test time. We introduce a training-free framework for incorporating such evidence into pretrained image-to-3D generative models without retraining or finetuning. To do this, we guide generation using a ray-consistent observation likelihood defined over the model's occupancy representation, combining surface occupancy and free-space evidence. Applied to SAM 3D and its multi-view extension, our approach substantially improves geometric fidelity across different levels of observability, as well as visual quality. Our results demonstrate that pretrained image-to-3D models can effectively integrate partial geometric observations through explicit test-time guidance, complementing their learned generative priors without modifying the underlying model.

</details>


### [80] [Programmable World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10540)
*Zheng-Hui Huang,Guixu Lin,Jiacheng Lin,Yi-Chuan Huang,Ruihan Yu,Muyao Niu,Siqi Yang,Yu-Lun Liu,Yung-Yu Chuang,Kaipeng Zhang,Zhixiang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Programmable World Model 框架，通过解耦世界状态演化与视觉生成，实现对虚拟世界的可编程控制。该框架利用自然语言指令生成可执行程序，精确管理实体状态与交互规则，并通过轻量级引擎维护持久的全局世界状态。采用状态增强的3D定向边界框作为中间表示，结合相机轨迹生成确定性的时空条件信号，驱动预训练视频模型进行视觉渲染。实验表明，该方法在 CombatStateBench 基准上达到 94% 的计数准确率和 98% 的状态准确率，显著优于现有方法，支持长时间、连贯的生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型缺乏可靠的持久世界状态维护机制和可编程规则控制能力，难以实现长期交互中的状态一致性与可预测性。

Method: 将世界状态演化与视觉生成解耦；通过自然语言指令生成可执行程序以定义实体状态与状态转移规则；使用轻量级引擎维护显式、持久的全局世界状态；引入状态增强的3D OBB作为中间表示，结合相机轨迹生成像素对齐的时空条件信号，输入预训练视频模型进行渲染。

Result: 在 CombatStateBench 上实现 94% 的计数准确率和 98% 的状态准确率，显著优于现有交互式视频世界模型，支持长时间、连贯的生成，验证了状态与渲染分离设计的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将显式世界状态演化与生成渲染分离，该框架实现了对虚拟世界的可编程、持久化控制，为构建具有规则性和可预测性的交互式虚拟环境提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Recent video world models generate increasingly realistic and interactive visual experiences, yet lack reliable mechanisms for maintaining persistent world state and enforcing programmable rules over extended interactions. We introduce Programmable World Model, a framework that decouples world-state evolution from visual observation generation. An agent translates natural-language instructions into executable programs that specify entity states and state-transition rules, enabling direct control over individual entities and their interactions. A lightweight engine executes these programs to update and maintain an explicit, persistent global world state, including off-screen entities and non-visual attributes. To connect world state with visual generation, we introduce state-augmented 3D oriented bounding boxes (OBBs) as an intermediate representation. This representation, together with the target camera trajectory, is deterministically compiled into pixel-aligned spatiotemporal conditioning signals for a pretrained video model serving as the generative renderer. This design allows users to create playable games with predefined mechanics, direct control over individual entities, and persistent world state throughout gameplay. We further introduce CombatStateBench, a benchmark for evaluating programmable world models. On CombatStateBench, our method achieves 94% Count Accuracy and 98% State Accuracy, substantially outperforming existing interactive video world models while supporting coherent long-horizon generation. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of separating explicit state evolution from generative rendering for building persistent, programmable worlds.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [81] [X-CoSD: Communication-Efficient Cross-Vocabulary Collaborative Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09166)
*Jaeduk Lee,Wan Choi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为X-CoSD的跨词汇协同推测解码框架，用于解决异构小语言模型（SLM）与大语言模型（LLM）之间因词汇不一致导致的通信开销和性能损失问题。通过混合重采样（HR）机制，将残差重采样过程分布在设备端的共用词汇区域和服务器端的专属词汇区域，仅需传输共用词汇部分的概率分布，显著降低通信负载。进一步提出的X-CoSD-E版本采用服务器重采样+设备验证机制，仅由服务器发送候选词及其概率供设备本地验证，实现更高效的通信。理论证明二者均能保持服务器LLM的生成分布，实验表明其在大幅提升生成速度的同时，维持了与服务器LLM相当的生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协同推测解码（CoSD）方法假设小语言模型（SLM）与大语言模型（LLM）共享词汇表，导致在残差重采样过程中需要频繁交换完整的词分布，造成较高的通信开销。此外，在实际应用中，SLM与LLM往往使用不同词汇表（异构词汇），因此亟需一种无需共享词汇、且通信高效的解码框架。

Method: 提出X-CoSD框架，基于混合重采样（HR）策略，将残差重采样任务分解为：设备端处理共用词汇部分，服务器端处理仅属于LLM的词汇部分，从而只需传输共用词汇的概率分布；进一步设计X-CoSD-E，采用服务器生成替换候选词及其概率，由设备进行本地验证，减少通信量。两者均保证最终输出分布与原始服务器LLM一致。

Result: 实验结果表明，X-CoSD和X-CoSD-E在保持与服务器LLM相当生成质量的前提下，显著提升了推理速度，并大幅降低了通信开销，尤其在异构词汇场景下表现优越。

Conclusion: X-CoSD和X-CoSD-E是高效、无损且通信友好的协同推测解码方案，适用于异构SLM-LLM系统，在保障生成质量的同时实现了高性能与低通信成本的平衡。

Abstract: This paper investigates collaborative speculative decoding (CoSD), a distributed large language model (LLM) inference framework in which an on-device small language model (SLM) drafts candidate tokens and a server LLM verifies them. Existing CoSD methods assume a shared vocabulary between the SLM and the LLM and incur substantial communication load because residual resampling requires token distribution exchange between the user device and the edge server. To address these limitations, we propose cross-vocabulary CoSD (X-CoSD), a lossless and communication-efficient CoSD framework for heterogeneous SLM-LLM vocabularies. X-CoSD is built on hybrid resampling (HR), which splits residual resampling across the common-vocabulary region on the device and the LLM-only region on the server, so that distribution transmission is required only for the common-vocabulary region. We further propose X-CoSD-E, an enhanced variant based on server resampling with device verification (SR-DV), in which the server sends only replacement candidates sampled from the server LLM and their corresponding probabilities for local verification at the device. We prove that both X-CoSD and X-CoSD-E preserve the server LLM distribution, and experiments show that they significantly improve token generation speed while maintaining generation quality comparable to that of the server LLM.

</details>


### [82] [StochBench: A Domain-Specific Benchmark for Stochastic Processes in Lean](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09264)
*Idan Davidovich,Debargha Ganguly,Vikash Singh,Vipin Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: StochBench 是一个基于 Lean 4 的 450 道研究生级随机过程问题的基准测试集，涵盖马尔可夫链、鞅、布朗运动等多个领域，每个问题均配有自然语言描述。使用 Opus 4.8 模型的智能体在每题 15 分钟限制下取得 34.9%（157/450）的证明率，优于现有数学竞赛类小规模基准，更贴近应用数学领域，同时保持对高级证明器的挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有形式化定理证明的基准测试多来自数学竞赛（如 IMO、Putnam），难以代表实际应用数学领域。随机过程作为重要应用数学分支，在 Mathlib 中覆盖不足，亟需专门的高质量基准来推动大语言模型在该领域的研究。

Method: 构建包含 450 道研究生级别随机过程问题的 StochBench 基准，问题涵盖有限与可数马尔可夫链、更新过程、随机游走、鞅、停时、排队、布朗运动、随机微积分、弱收敛、泊松过程及连续时间马尔可夫过程等，所有问题均配有自然语言源文本，并基于 Lean 4 实现。采用 Opus 4.8 模型驱动的智能体进行自动证明实验。

Result: Opus 4.8 基于 StochBench 的智能体在每题 15 分钟限制下实现 34.9% 的证明成功率（157/450），表明该基准既具有挑战性又具备现实代表性，有效填补了当前数学形式化领域中应用数学方向的空白。

Conclusion: StochBench 提供了一个高保真、多样化且具有挑战性的随机过程形式化证明基准，能更真实地反映领域特定应用数学的需求，为评估和提升大语言模型在复杂数学推理中的能力提供了新标准。

Abstract: Leading benchmarks for formal theorem proving with large language models are small collections drawn from competition math, such as the IMO and Putnam, that poorly represent field-specific applications. We introduce StochBench, a Lean 4 benchmark of 450 graduate stochastic-processes problems at varying abstraction levels, each paired with its natural-language source. Addressing a field underrepresented in Mathlib, it covers finite and countable Markov chains, renewal processes, random walks, martingales, stopping times, queues, Brownian motion, stochastic calculus, weak convergence, and Poisson and continuous-time Markov processes. Our Opus 4.8-based agent achieves a 34.9% proof rate (157/450) under a 15-minute per-problem limit. StochBench better represents domain-specific applied mathematics while remaining challenging for advanced provers.

</details>


### [83] [Osprey: Target-agnostic Pre-training Makes Stronger Drafters in Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09338)
*Fengxiang Bie,Yuqing Jian,Yifan Yu,Zhongzhu Zhou,Zelei Shao,Ben Athiwaratkun,Shuaiwen Leon Song,Chenfeng Xu,Xiaoxia Wu,Tianyi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Osprey提出一种新的预训练方法，用于生成可泛化的草稿模型（drafters），以提升大语言模型（LLM）推理中的推测解码速度。传统方法需为每个目标模型单独训练草稿模型，导致泛化能力差且效率低。Osprey通过复用通用的小型预训练语言模型作为基础，仅通过轻量级适配步骤即可迁移至不同目标模型，显著提升接受率和推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推测解码方法中，草稿模型通常针对单一目标模型进行训练，导致在负载变化时接受率急剧下降，缺乏泛化能力。而现代大模型的核心优势正是其广泛的泛化能力，因此需要一种能够继承这种能力的草稿模型训练方式。

Method: Osprey采用预训练的小型语言模型作为初始骨架，通过剪枝降低深度以满足延迟要求；恢复其语言建模能力通过目标无关的下一词预训练；并通过词汇对齐、零初始化QKV扩展和从目标模型输出分布的蒸馏，实现对各目标模型的轻量级适配。

Result: 实验表明，单个预训练的Osprey骨架可在多个目标模型间有效迁移，平均接受长度提升16.1%（Qwen3-8B）、21.2%（Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct）和22.7%（MiniMax-M2.5），同时每秒处理字数提高17.5%，尤其在跨领域和多语言数据上表现更优。

Conclusion: Osprey成功将预训练视为可复用的目标无关资产，大幅降低为新目标模型训练草稿模型的成本，实现了高效、泛化能力强的推测解码，为大规模语言模型推理加速提供了新范式。

Abstract: Speculative decoding is critical for accelerating LLM inference. However, the speedup is fragile: drafters are typically trained against a narrow distribution for a single target model, and their acceptance rate collapses under workload shifts. This is a striking inversion of modern LLM development, where target models are valued precisely for the broad generalization they acquire through large-scale pretraining. We argue that the natural remedy, pretraining, has been hard to apply to drafters because existing recipes are target-specific: the drafter consumes the target's hidden states and is distilled on the target's logits, so pretraining must be repeated for each target. We introduce Osprey, which instead bootstraps drafters from off-the-shelf pretrained small language models, treating broad pretraining as a reusable, target-agnostic asset and reducing per-target work to a lightweight adaptation step. Realizing this requires overcoming two challenges: small LMs are far deeper than a latency-bound drafter can afford, and their pretrained computation must remain intact while the drafter learns to ingest target hidden states and emit tokens in the target's vocabulary. Osprey addresses both by pruning to a shallow backbone, restoring its language-modeling capability with target-agnostic next-token pretraining, and adapting it to each target through vocabulary alignment, zero-initialized QKV expansion, and distillation from the target model's output distribution. Empirically, a single pretrained Osprey backbone transfers across targets and improves mean acceptance length by 16.1% for Qwen3-8B, 21.2% for Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct, and 22.7% for the 229B MiniMax-M2.5 (with 17.5% higher tokens per second), with the largest gains on out-of-domain and multilingual data. Our code is available at https://github.com/LeanModels/Osprey.

</details>


### [84] [SWORD: Wikidata-based Distortions Reveal Hidden Cross-Lingual Inconsistencies in LLM Factual Error Rejection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09349)
*Sanghyeok Park,Minji Kang,Hosung Kwak,Jinhyuk Yun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SWORD基准，通过在八种语言中对Wikidata三元组进行受控扰动生成语法正确但事实错误的陈述，评估模型在跨语言环境中拒绝事实错误的一致性。发现模型在语义上合理的错误上表现优于随机错误，表明其依赖分布熟悉度而非真实事实验证；且在（东）亚洲语言中性能显著下降，跨语言差距达28个百分点，揭示多语言事实推理存在不对称能力，被传统指标掩盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要奖励正确答案选择，而非真正评估模型对事实的理解能力，尤其在多语言场景下无法揭示模型在不同语言间的真实推理差异。

Method: 基于Wikidata三元组，通过随机实体替换和语义合理属性选择等控制扰动方式，在八种广泛使用的语言中生成语法正确但事实错误的陈述，构建SWORD基准以系统评估模型在跨语言情境下的事实错误拒绝能力。

Result: 1. 模型在语义合理的错误上准确率高于随机错误，表明其依赖分布熟悉而非真实事实验证；2. 多语言模型在（东）亚洲语言上的性能显著下降，跨语言性能差距最高达28个百分点（49%相对减少），暴露出多语言事实推理中的不对称能力。

Conclusion: 标准多语言评估指标会掩盖模型在事实推理方面的跨语言不对称能力，SWORD揭示了模型在真实事实理解上的局限性，强调需要更精细的跨语言评估框架来推动真正多语言智能的发展。

Abstract: Modern LLMs demonstrate impressive multilingual performance, yet standard benchmarks primarily reward selecting correct answers rather than evaluating genuine factual understanding. We introduce Systematic Wikidata-based Object-Relation Distortion (SWORD), a benchmark that evaluates whether models consistently reject factual errors across languages. SWORD generates syntactically well-formed but factually incorrect statements in eight widely spoken languages through controlled perturbations of Wikidata triples, ranging from random entity substitutions to semantically plausible property-based selections. Our distortion-based evaluation surfaces two critical insights that remain entirely obscured by conventional benchmarks. First, models counterintuitively achieve higher accuracy on semantically plausible distortions than on nonsensical random substitutions, suggesting reliance on distributional familiarity rather than genuine factual verification. Second, models exhibiting comparable baseline accuracy across languages show substantial performance degradation specifically on (East) Asian languages when presented with distorted statements, with cross-lingual performance gaps reaching up to 28 percentage points (49\% relative reduction) in some models. These findings demonstrate that multilingual factual reasoning involves asymmetric capabilities that aggregate accuracy metrics systematically obscure.

</details>


### [85] [Auditable Emergency Triage for Maternal and Newborn Care in India](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09356)
*Shobhit Jagga,Aman Dalmia,Niharika Priyadarshini,Neelima Devadas,Amrita K Prasen,Nikhil Nalin,Santhosh SJ,Sreeram Nurani Ramasubramanian,Muhammed Afeer K,Anubhav Arora*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究针对医疗咨询平台Noora Health中护士处理紧急情况分诊的挑战，提出一种结合大语言模型（LLM）与确定性规则引擎的新系统。原系统依赖黑箱式LLM判断紧急程度，难以审计且评估成本高。新方法将分诊分为两步：首先由LLM基于临床专家编写的术语表提取症状和患者背景；其次由规则引擎判定是否为紧急情况。该设计显著提升召回率（0.565→0.810）和F1值（0.606→0.702），同时实现可解释性和可维护性，临床人员可独立添加规则并快速验证，无需重新评估整个系统。自部署以来已处理超过15万条消息，紧急事件识别准确，过激率仅17.8%，无漏诊，且已新增48条规则，证明系统具备高效迭代能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始系统依赖黑箱大语言模型进行紧急分诊，导致错误分析困难、评估成本高，且缺乏对临床决策逻辑的显式表达，难以持续优化和审计。

Method: 将紧急分诊任务分解为两个阶段：第一阶段使用由临床专家制定的词汇表，让大语言模型提取标准化的症状和患者背景信息；第二阶段通过确定性规则引擎根据预定义的临床场景判断是否为紧急情况。

Result: 系统召回率从0.565提升至0.810，F1分数从0.606提升至0.702。规则引擎贡献了主要性能提升，同时实现了可审计性。部署后处理152,421条查询，识别出28,535例紧急情况（占比18.7%），过激率17.8%，未出现漏诊。临床团队已新增48条规则，表明系统支持快速迭代。

Conclusion: 通过将大语言模型与可解释的规则引擎结合，该系统在保持高准确性的同时显著提升了系统的可审计性、可维护性和临床可接受性，为大规模医疗咨询中的智能分诊提供了可行、可持续的解决方案。

Abstract: At Noora Health, our nurses answer more than 50,000 medical queries per month on our WhatsApp-based service that provides caregivers with on-demand support. Their most time-critical task is emergency triage: deciding which queries need immediate in-person attention. To support them, we built a system that uses a large language model (LLM) to classify whether a message is an emergency and provide a rationale for interpretability. But the system was opaque: analyzing mistakes meant reading reasoning chains for each message, which is infeasible at our scale. Prompt changes meant re-running a full evaluation to prevent regressions, which was both costly and operationally challenging. Clinicians follow a decision tree to make this call, but it was never documented or passed to the model, which relied on a flat list of danger signs. To address these issues, we decomposed triage into two steps: an LLM extracts canonical symptoms and patient context from the query using a clinician-authored vocabulary, and a deterministic rule engine captures the scenarios that indicate an emergency. We show that the new system raised recall from 0.565 to 0.810 and F1 from 0.606 to 0.702, with structured rules driving most of the accuracy gains while the decomposition provides auditability: clinical experts can inspect each stage of the new system to see whether the query was mistranslated, symptoms were incorrectly extracted, patient context was wrongly inferred, or the necessary rules were missing. They can add new rules independently without causing regressions and avoid running costly evaluations. Since deployment, the new system has triaged 152,421 patient queries and flagged 28,535 (18.7%) as emergencies. The over-escalation rate has been 17.8%, without any increase in missed emergencies. Clinicians have also added 48 new rules since deployment, evidence of the faster correction loop we set out to build.

</details>


### [86] [Do LLMs Make More Mistakes If They Do Not Believe the Input Data?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09363)
*Peter Kochelka,Aleš Manuel Papáček,Vojtěch Dvořák,Ondřej Dušek*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了大语言模型（LLMs）在检索增强生成或数据到文本系统中因幻觉或误解事实而影响可用性的问题，重点关注模型对所提供上下文的忠实度如何受上下文可信度影响（即上下文-记忆冲突）。通过在英语、捷克语、斯洛伐克语和上索布语中基于本地捷克和斯洛伐克数据的三类知识图谱三元组（真实、反事实、虚构）进行生成实验，发现人类标注样本中上下文-记忆冲突较弱；即使使用反事实输入，模型的忠实度评分也仅比真实输入低0.05分（1-5分制），且不当的模型评判者会导致对冲突强度的高估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在生成过程中容易产生幻觉或误读事实，尤其是在依赖外部信息的场景中，这严重影响其可靠性。为理解这种错误模式，研究聚焦于上下文与模型内部知识之间的冲突，特别是当输入内容来自非主流语言或低资源语言时，模型可能因缺乏充分的先验知识而更易出错。

Method: 研究采用真实（FA）、反事实（CFA）和虚构（FI）的RDF三元组作为输入，覆盖英语、捷克语、斯洛伐克语和上索布语，利用大语言模型生成文本，并以人工标注为基准，评估模型生成内容的忠实度。同时引入大语言模型（Kimi K3）作为评判者，比较其与人类判断的一致性，并分析不同评判者的偏差。

Result: 实验结果显示，在人类标注样本中，上下文-记忆冲突效应较弱，反事实输入的忠实度仅略低于真实输入（-0.05分）。此外，若选择不合适的模型作为评判者，会显著高估上下文-记忆冲突的严重程度。

Conclusion: 大语言模型在面对反事实或虚构上下文时表现出较强的鲁棒性，上下文-记忆冲突并不如预期强烈。研究强调了选择合适评判标准的重要性，避免因评判者偏差导致对模型性能的误判。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are prone to hallucinating or misinterpreting facts, which impairs their usability in retrieval-augmented generation or data-to-text systems. We analyse how faithfulness of LLMs to provided context depends on how plausible they perceive the context to be (context-memory conflict). To better identify error patterns, we make use of the increased difficulty of non-English and low-resource language text generation and input data based on local knowledge, only partially captured in models' parametric knowledge. We let the models generate text in English, Czech, Slovak and Upper Sorbian from factual (FA), counterfactual (CFA) and fictional (FI) RDF triples containing local Czech and Slovak data. Contrary to our expectations, we observe only a weak context-memory conflict on the human-annotated sample. For Kimi K3 as an LLM judge, which agrees well with human annotations on the sample, counterfactual inputs receive only slightly lower faithfulness scores than factual ones (-0.05 on a 1-5 scale). We also find that a suboptimal choice of LLM judge would lead to overestimating the strength of the context-memory conflict.

</details>


### [87] [Benchmarking Hybrid Deep Research Across Database Querying and Web Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09410)
*Ruofan Wu,Peiran Xu,Xiaolong Li,Fan Shu,Soyoung Yoon,Yite Wang,Xiaodong Yu,Boyi Liu,Feng Yan,Debiao Li,Yuxiong He,Zhewei Yao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出首个融合网页搜索与SQL查询的深度研究基准HybridDeepResearch，评估代理在结构化与非结构化数据间传递信息的能力。包含380个依赖工具的任务，覆盖三种推理模式，验证通过自动化与人工审查。现有顶尖模型在困难子集上仅达50-54% Pass@8，表明跨模态约束保持仍是重大挑战。代码与数据已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准孤立评估网页搜索与数据库查询，忽视了真实任务中多环境证据整合的关键‘交接’环节，即在不同系统间传递信息时保持约束的能力。

Method: 构建HybridDeepResearch基准，包含380个工具依赖任务，基于LiveSQLBench-Base-Lite数据库和公开网络语料，涵盖SQL2S、S2SQL和Parallel三种推理模式，并通过自动化检查与人工评审确保质量。

Result: 即使最先进的模型如GLM-5.2、Claude-Sonnet-4.6和GPT-5，在困难子集上的Pass@8也仅为50-54%，且方向性推理远难于并行交叉推理，凸显跨数据空间保持约束的难度。

Conclusion: 当前智能体在整合结构化与非结构化信息时仍面临严重挑战，尤其在维持约束条件方面，亟需新方法突破。

Abstract: While autonomous agents have made significant strides in "deep research" by iteratively navigating the open web to synthesize information, real-world problem-solving is rarely confined to a single environment. Complex analytical tasks inherently require agents to weave together evidence from both ambiguous unstructured text (e.g., the open web) and highly precise structured data (e.g., relational databases). However, existing benchmarks evaluate these modalities in isolation, failing to capture the critical "handoff" - the ability to preserve constraints when moving evidence between systems. We introduce HybridDeepResearch, to our knowledge the first deep-research benchmark that requires both web search and SQL to form a complete, verifiable answer. The benchmark contains 380 tool-dependent tasks grounded in LiveSQLBench-Base-Lite databases and public web corpora, validated through automated checks and human review, and covering three reasoning patterns: SQL2S, S2SQL, and Parallel. Evaluations across proprietary and open-weight models under various agentic scaffolds reveal that even state-of-the-art models like GLM-5.2, Claude-Sonnet-4.6 and GPT-5 achieve only about 50-54% Pass@8 on the hard subset. Notably, results show that directional reasoning is substantially more difficult than parallel intersection, highlighting that bridging structured and unstructured information spaces without losing constraints remains a major open challenge for agentic systems. Code and datasets are publicly available at GitHub (https://github.com/Snowflake-AI-Research/HybridDeepResearch) and Hugging Face (https://huggingface.co/datasets/Snowflake/HybridDeepResearch).

</details>


### [88] [Edu-QuRating: Multi-Dimensional Educational Data Curation with Distilled Pairwise Judgements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09425)
*Oliver G. B. Garrod,Robin A. A. Ince,Meng Liu,Mohamed Huti,Moritz Boos,Amy Waldock,Dominic Andrews,Paul Atherton*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Edu-QuRating，一种多维度教育数据评分与筛选流水线，通过定义教育特定评估标准，利用大模型判断文档对，并将偏好信息提炼为可复用的Edu-QuRater，实现对文本片段在六个教育维度上的评分。该方法在多项任务中表现优异，显著提升小模型预训练效果和后训练阶段的教学质量与指令遵循能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有教育数据过滤方法将教育价值视为单一标量属性，难以满足高密度教育数据集中的精细化需求；优质学习材料需具备准确性、吸引力、结构良好及受众适配性等多重特征，因此需要更精细的多维度评估机制。

Method: 设计教育专用评分框架，采用大模型进行文档对的成对偏好标注，通过偏好蒸馏生成可独立使用的Edu-QuRater模型，用于对单个文本块进行多维度教育质量评分。

Result: 最佳Edu-QuRater在六项教育标准上对GPT-4.1-mini的成对判断回收率达到0.917的平均准确率；应用于数据筛选时，提升了小模型在九个基准测试上的综合表现，尤其在特定任务中增益明显；用于GRPO后训练时，结合教育评分与答案结构奖励，生成的响应在教学质量和指令遵循上优于Qwen3-4B基线模型。

Conclusion: Edu-QuRating有效实现了教育数据的多维度质量评估与筛选，显著提升了语言模型在教育场景下的预训练与后训练性能，为高质量教育内容的自动化处理提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Educational data filters have become a practical way to improve language-model pre-training, but most filters treat educational value as a single scalar property. This may be too broad for some applications, especially if the data set already features a high density of educational material. Useful learning material needs to be accurate, engaging, well structured, and appropriate for the intended audience and application (e.g. learner- vs teacher-facing). Following QuRating (Wettig et al. 2024), we introduce Edu-QuRating: a pipeline for multi-dimensional educational data scoring and curation. Edu-QuRating defines education-specific rubrics, uses an LLM judge to label sampled document pairs and distills those pairwise preferences into reusable Edu-QuRaters, which can score individual text chunks on a set of educational criteria. Across two sequence-classification base models and six educational criteria, the best Edu-QuRater recovers held-out GPT-4.1-mini pairwise judgements with mean accuracy 0.917. We then apply the resulting scorers in two applications. First, we investigate the potential of Edu-QuRaters for corpus filtering to improve pretraining of small language models. We scored 322.25M FineWeb-Edu-Fortified documents to obtain a filtered pre-training mixture. In matched single-run pre-training comparisons, models trained with Edu-QuRating-based mixtures reached higher observed aggregate accuracy across nine benchmarks than the FineWeb-Edu baseline, with gains concentrated in particular tasks. Second, we used Edu-QuRater scores as reward terms for GRPO post-training. In held-out pairwise judge evaluations, combining Edu-QuRater and answer-structure rewards produced responses preferred to the Qwen3-4B base model on both pedagogical quality and instruction following.

</details>


### [89] [The Mutations of Machine Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09496)
*Mauricio Figueroa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨算法输出的法律基础与社会影响，揭示机器话语的三次演变：搜索引擎将话语转化为可查询数据，社交媒体将表达重构为注意力指标，而生成式对话系统则带来复杂的技法律纠缠与认知后果。文章旨在整合碎片化讨论，为法律与跨学科研究者提供理解算法言论的分析框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前算法输出深刻塑造数字生活，但其法律角色常被忽视。学界虽关注自由表达、隐私与传播问题，但对法律如何构成这些技术过程仍缺乏系统性反思。本文旨在填补这一空白，推动对算法话语的深层法律思考。

Method: 通过梳理算法技术演进中的三重话语转变，结合法律、传播与技术研究，构建一个概念性分析框架，厘清算法输出的法律建构机制及其社会影响。

Result: 揭示了算法从信息检索到注意力经济再到生成式对话的演化路径，指出法律不仅是回应技术的工具，更是塑造算法话语的关键力量。提出一个整合性的分析视角，有助于法律与跨学科领域理解并应对算法时代的挑战。

Conclusion: 算法并非中立工具，其输出形态深受法律与制度设计影响。未来需超越被动监管思维，主动参与算法话语的法律建构，以维护公共利益与民主价值。

Abstract: Algorithmic outputs now populate the digital environments through which contemporary life is organized. The role of law in facilitating and constituting (rather than merely responding to) these processes is gaining increasing traction across scholarly accounts. This inquiry traces the evolution of algorithmic outputs attending to their legal underpinnings and social implications, surfacing the mutations of machine speech.
  The first mutation redefined speech as data to be queried: search engines transformed the web from a space of information retrieval into an economic regime of algorithmic visibility. The second mutation reframed speech as engagement: social media platforms fused moderation with amplification, turning expression into a metric of attention, governed by corporate architectures. The third mutation emerges in conversational systems and interfaces, where generative text displaces information retrieval, bringing with it dense technolegal entanglements and profound epistemic consequences.
  Scholars of freedom of expression, informational privacy, and communication studies have long grappled with these dynamics, yet their implications for broader legal thought have also become urgent. This piece seeks to organize and clarify the evolving debate around algorithmic speech, making this critical but often fragmented discourse more accessible to wider legal and interdisciplinary audiences. In doing so, it bridges the gap between observing technological transformation and critically assessing the constitutive role of law within it, offering a conceptual resource for researchers, students, policymakers, and practitioners navigating and contesting this evolving landscape.

</details>


### [90] [TEFM: Token-Efficient Faithful Modeling for Structured Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09552)
*Zhichao Hou,Lingdao Sha,Xueyu Mao,Yang Liu,Peijie Qiu,Rui Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TEFM框架通过压缩结构化数据为紧凑的Behavioral Code令牌，显著降低令牌消耗并保持高精度，在临床和安全领域分别实现约1%和2%的令牌保留率，同时生成忠实的推理依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大语言模型在关键领域应用中的两大障碍：令牌效率与忠实性。

Method: 提出TEFM框架，采用双保真度目标联合优化代码级重建与预测级保真度，实现结构化数据的高效压缩与忠实推理。

Result: 在多个领域数据集和模型（Qwen3、Gemma-2、Phi-4）上，TEFM在大幅减少令牌使用的同时保持了具有竞争力的分类准确率，并生成可信的推理过程。

Conclusion: TEFM有效解决了关键领域中大语言模型应用的令牌效率与忠实性问题，具备实际应用潜力。

Abstract: In this paper, we solve two fundamental obstacles in applying LLMs to critical domains: token efficiency and faithfulness. To address both constraints jointly, we present TEFM (Token-Efficient Faithful Modeling), a framework designed for structured data analysis in critical domains. TEFM achieves token efficiency by compressing lengthy structured observations into compact Behavioral Code tokens, dramatically reducing token consumption with minimal information loss. Moreover, TEFM enables faithful rationalization through a dual-fidelity objective that jointly optimizes code-level reconstruction and prediction-level fidelity, identifying minimal sufficient feature subsets grounded in input data. Comprehensive experiments across various domain datasets and model backbones (Qwen3, Gemma-2, Phi-4) show that TEFM achieves competitive classification accuracy with dramatic token reduction (approximately 1\% token retention in clinical and 2\% in security domains) while producing faithful rationales.

</details>


### [91] [BuzzASR: A Swarm of 100+ Monolingual Speech Recognition Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09554)
*Shivam Singh,Aditya Yadavalli,Catherine Arnett,Alex Warstadt*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BuzzASR 是一个针对 102 种语言的 Whisper 模型微调集合，通过简单微调和更复杂的字典替换与数据增强策略，显著提升低资源语言的自动语音识别性能。在 FLEURS 和 Common Voice 数据集上，其平均字符错误率（CER）降低超过 2.8 倍，部分语言达到开源系统最优水平，并实现最高达 21.7 倍的压缩率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主流 ASR 模型如 Whisper 虽然具备强大的多语言能力，但对低资源语言表现不佳。为提升这些语言的识别效果，需进行有效的语言适应，而大规模的语言专项微调尚属空白。

Method: 采用大规模单语数据微调，结合字典替换与文本仅微调的数据增强策略，构建语言特化模型。

Result: 在 102 个语言中，77 个优于 Whisper-large-v3；平均 CER 降低超 2.8 倍；27 个语言达到开源系统最佳水平；字典压缩率平均提升 3.3 倍，最高达 21.7 倍。

Conclusion: BuzzASR 证明了大规模语言特化微调的有效性，显著提升了低资源语言的 ASR 性能，同时实现了更高的编码效率，为多语言语音识别提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: We introduce BuzzASR, a collection of language-specialized fine-tuned Whisper models adapted for automatic speech recognition (ASR) in 102 languages. Large end-to-end Transformer-based ASR models such as Whisper have revolutionized ASR, but most prominent models are highly multilingual. As a result, these models often perform poorly on languages less well-represented in their training set. While it has long been known that effective language adaptation can be achieved through simple fine-tuning on monolingual data, this strategy has only been applied to a small number of languages. We massively scale up this simple approach to 102 languages covered in the FLEURS dataset, while also implementing a more complex language adaptation strategy that integrates monolingual tokenizer replacement and data augmentation using text-only fine-tuning. BuzzASR models outperform Whisper-large-v3 on 77 out of 102 languages, reducing character error rates (CER) by a factor of over 2.8 on average. Our models achieve state-of-the-art CER among open-source systems on 27 of 102 languages on the combined FLEURS and Common Voice test set. Our tokenizer replacement strategy yields an average 3.3x improvement in compression rate (characters per token) over Whisper's multilingual BPE, with gains of up to 21.7x. We release all models, code, and detailed results: https://lemn-lab.github.io/buzz-asr

</details>


### [92] [Towards Automatic Evolution Tree Generation from Citation Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09561)
*Zexing Zhao,Yuntong Hu,Liang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EvoTree 是一种分阶段框架，用于构建更准确的 AI 子领域方法演化树。它通过分离概念骨架学习与时间细化，克服了现有方法在处理过渡性论文和拓扑倒置上的问题。该方法首先使用基于图的编码器和分布聚类生成稳定的概念骨架，再通过时间微调将边缘论文合理归入内部节点，最后利用大语言模型标注概念而不改变结构。研究还发布了首个涵盖11个AI子领域的标注基准。EvoTree 在NMI、引用方向准确性、概念纯度等指标上优于所有基线，并且是唯一能有效识别边缘论文的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献综述难以应对快速出版节奏；现有分类方法多为叶节点导向且忽略时间信息，导致过渡性论文被错误归类或产生拓扑倒置。需要一种能同时捕捉概念演化与时间路径的方法。

Method: EvoTree 采用三阶段流程：(1) 使用图感知编码器结合分布聚类构建稳定的概念骨架；(2) 基于单调路径约束进行时间微调，重新定位边缘论文至内部节点；(3) 通过大语言模型对概念进行标注，保持拓扑不变。

Result: EvoTree 在多个指标上超越所有基线，包括最高的NMI值、引用方向准确性、概念纯度；在标注数据集上首次实现非平凡的边缘论文检测能力。

Conclusion: EvoTree 提供了一种高效、可扩展且具有时间一致性的方法来构建AI子领域的方法演化树，显著提升了分类的准确性和可解释性，为文献综述自动化提供了新范式。

Abstract: Surveys remain the primary way researchers grasp the lineage of methods within an AI subfield, but they scale poorly against the current rate of publication. Existing taxonomy-induction methods are largely leaf-bound and time-agnostic; they tend to force transitional papers into mature leaves and can create topological inversions between ancestors and descendants. We propose EvoTree, a staged framework that decouples conceptual backbone learning from temporal refinement: a graph-aware encoder with distribution-based hierarchical clustering yields a stable taxonomy backbone; temporal fine-tuning then re-attaches marginal papers to internal nodes under monotonic-path constraints; a final LLM pass labels concepts without altering the topology. We release the first annotated benchmark for this task across 11 AI subfields. EvoTree attains the highest NMI and citation-direction accuracy among all baselines and the best concept purity on the annotated benchmark, and is the only method with non-trivial marginal-paper detection on the annotated set.

</details>


### [93] [Reproducing Omitted Temporal Expressions in Japanese News for Retrieval-Augmented Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09569)
*Tomoaki Yasuda,Shotaro Ishihara*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出jaROTE，一个基于规则的日本新闻时间表达式补全管道，利用发布日期作为外部上下文，恢复省略的时间表达（如仅含月份或日期）为具体日期或时间段。实验表明，jaROTE在两个新闻语料库上表现优异，性能优于传统方法且在与大语言模型对比中仍具竞争力，同时显著提升受限时间的词汇检索效果，验证了基于发布时间的时间归一化在日文新闻检索中的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新闻文章常省略完整的时间表达（如仅提及月份或日期），若在搜索和检索增强生成系统中作为独立文本处理，缺乏上下文会导致时间解释不一致或错误。因此需要利用发布日期来推断并还原缺失的时间信息，以提高时间敏感任务的准确性。

Method: 基于对日文新闻的分析，结合已有的时间表达提取与归一化技术，构建了一个规则驱动的pipeline——jaROTE，用于根据文章发布日期补全省略的时间表达，将其转化为具体日期或时间区间。

Result: jaROTE在两个日文新闻语料库上表现出高精度，其性能优于传统方法，并在与大语言模型的比较中保持竞争力；此外，经过时间补全后的文本显著提升了时间约束下的词汇检索效果。

Conclusion: 基于发布日期的时间表达补全对于提升日文新闻在检索和RAG系统中的稳定性与准确性具有重要价值，jaROTE提供了一种快速、低成本且高效的方法，适用于实际应用场景。

Abstract: News articles often contain omitted temporal expressions, such as day-only or month-only mentions, which must be interpreted with reference to the publication date. When such articles are indexed or processed as standalone text in search and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems, these omissions can cause temporal mismatches and unstable interpretation by large language models. We focus on reproducing omitted temporal expressions as concrete dates or intervals using the publication date as external context before the articles are indexed for search and RAG applications. Specifically, building on established temporal-expression extraction and normalization techniques and informed by a manual analysis of Japanese news articles, we propose jaROTE, a rule-based pipeline for Japanese news. Experiments on two news corpora demonstrate that jaROTE achieves high performance, and remains competitive with LLMs while providing a fast, low-cost pipeline. We further show that temporal reproduction improves time-constrained lexical retrieval, demonstrating the practical value of publication-date-grounded normalization for Japanese news retrieval.

</details>


### [94] [Beyond Top Words: MonoTM for Topic Modeling with Interpretable Monosemantic Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09575)
*Una Joh,Bei Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MonoTM is an interpretable topic modeling framework that decouples document--topic mixture estimation from semantic interpretation using sparse autoencoders (SAEs). It uses full SAE features for mixture estimation and a separate set of corpus-grounded semantic features for topic descriptors, improving interpretability without sacrificing global topic structure.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing topic models rely on top-ranked words, which offer limited semantic representation. While SAEs can extract interpretable features from dense representations, the relationship between feature interpretability and topic-inference quality is unclear. MonoTM aims to address this by separating mixture estimation from semantic description.

Method: MonoTM employs two stages: first, it estimates document--topic mixtures using the full bag-of-features from SAEs; second, it learns topic descriptors using a distinct vocabulary of corpus-grounded semantic features, preserving global topic structure while enhancing interpretability.

Result: On three benchmark corpora, MonoTM demonstrates that different SAE configurations and feature subsets optimize either mixture estimation or semantic interpretation. The framework successfully maintains strong topic structure while providing more meaningful topic descriptions than individual words.

Conclusion: MonoTM effectively decouples topic mixture estimation from semantic interpretation, enabling more interpretable and useful topic representations for downstream corpus analysis.

Abstract: Topic models summarize large text corpora, but top-ranked words often provide only a limited representation of topic semantics. Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) offer a way to move beyond word-level descriptors by extracting interpretable features from dense representations, yet how feature interpretability relates to topic-inference quality remains unclear. We introduce \textbf{MonoTM}, an interpretable topic modeling framework that decouples these roles. Across three benchmark corpora, we show that document--topic mixture estimation and semantic interpretation favor different SAE configurations and feature subsets. MonoTM estimates mixtures from the full SAE bag-of-features representation and, with them fixed, learns topic descriptors over a separate vocabulary of corpus-grounded semantic features. This design preserves global topic structure while representing topics with semantic units more meaningful than individual words, making them more useful for downstream corpus analysis.

</details>


### [95] [SEA-SpeechBench: A Large-Scale Multitask Benchmark for Speech Understanding Across Southeast Asia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09672)
*Jingyi Liao,Wenyu Zhang,Zhuohan Liu,Yingxu He,Geyu Lin,Xunlong Zou,Shuo Sun,Syed Ali Redha Alsagoff,Ai Ti Aw*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SEA-SpeechBench是首个针对11种东南亚语言的大规模多任务语音理解基准，涵盖97,194个样本、597小时音频数据和99个评估集，包含9项任务，覆盖语音处理、副语言分析和时间理解三大类。研究发现当前模型在时间理解、情绪识别和语音翻译方面表现不佳，低资源语言（如缅甸语、泰米尔语）提示性能比英语低达41个百分点，凸显了模型在非英语语境下的局限性，强调需推动更具包容性的模型开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音与多模态大模型的评估框架主要以英语为中心，导致东南亚（SEA）语言严重缺乏代表性，亟需一个全面且多样化的基准来评估这些语言的语音理解能力。

Method: 构建SEA-SpeechBench，整合97,194个样本、597小时音频数据，设计涵盖语音处理、副语言分析和时间理解的9项任务，采用母语和英语双语提示策略，对主流开源与专有系统进行评估。

Result: 所有模型在时间理解、情绪识别和语音翻译任务上表现均不理想；低资源语言（如缅甸语、泰米尔语）的提示性能相比英语下降最高达41个百分点，揭示了模型在非英语环境中的显著缺陷。

Conclusion: SEA-SpeechBench揭示了当前语音语言模型在东南亚语言上的重大性能短板，强调必须推进面向多元语言的包容性模型研发，以实现更公平、有效的语音理解技术。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of audio and multimodal large language models has unlocked transformative speech understanding capabilities, yet evaluation frameworks remain predominantly English-centric, leaving Southeast Asian (SEA) languages critically underrepresented. We introduce SEA-SpeechBench, to the best of our knowledge, the first large-scale multitask benchmark that evaluates speech understanding in 11 SEA languages through 97,194 samples across 99 evaluation sets and 597 hours of curated audio data. Our benchmark comprises 9 diverse tasks across 3 categories: speech processing (automatic speech recognition, speech translation, spoken question answering), paralinguistic analysis (emotion, gender, age, speaker recognition), and temporal understanding, a novel dimension featuring timestamped content queries and temporal localization within extended audio sequences up to 3 minutes. We implement multilingual prompting in both native SEA languages and English to reflect user interactions with audio-language models. Evaluation of leading open-source and proprietary systems reveals marked performance gaps. Across all models, performance remains underwhelming on temporal understanding, emotion recognition, and speech translation. Prompting in low-resource languages such as Burmese and Tamil lags behind English by up to 41 percentage points. Our findings expose critical model limitations and underscore the need for inclusive model development. The SEA-SpeechBench benchmark is available at https://zwenyu.github.io/SEA-SpeechBench/.

</details>


### [96] [X2-NativeCursor: Native-Token Text Progress Tracking for Incremental-Text Streaming Codec TTS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09677)
*Zehan Liu,Carl Chen,Rime Wen,Kaiqi Fu,Altman Lin,Shawn Qin,Lights Shi,Roy Gan,Hao Wang,Qian Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: X2-NativeCursor 是一种轻量级的进度追踪器，可在不改变 TTS 生成器的前提下，通过原始语音标记（native speech tokens）实时跟踪文本发音进度，适用于增量式流式语音合成。它利用文本与语音标记编码器结合局部匹配器估计当前发音位置，并通过输出规则确保光标不后退。在中文场景下，其平均绝对误差仅为0.151字符（80毫秒前瞻），显著优于基于波形的基线方法（1.253字符，320毫秒前瞻），同时大幅降低对实时性的开销。该方法已成功应用于 Qwen3-TTS 和 CosyVoice2，并公开了代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式文本到语音系统在同步高亮、中断处理和对话历史更新时，缺乏可靠的在线文本进度追踪机制。传统基于波形的对齐方法需要完整音频或实时声学处理，延迟高且资源消耗大。因此亟需一种低延迟、轻量级的进度追踪方案，能在语音生成前阶段直接从语音标记中推断发音位置。

Method: 提出 X2-NativeCursor：一个无需修改 TTS 生成器的轻量级观察器。其核心包括：1）归一化策略，将发音标签与其原始文本片段关联；2）文本编码器与原生语音标记编码器联合建模；3）局部匹配器估算当前标签位置；4）输出规则将可变的位置估计转换为不可后退的稳定光标。

Result: 在中文测试中，X2-NativeCursor 的平均绝对误差为 0.151 字符（80ms 前瞻），远低于基线方法的 1.253 字符（320ms 前瞻）。实时性方面，对齐的实时因子从 0.3598 降至 0.0180。即使在更严格的自动对齐参考下，性能依然保持优越。验证了其在 Qwen3-TTS 和 CosyVoice2 上的通用性与可迁移性。

Conclusion: X2-NativeCursor 实现了高效、低延迟、稳定的流式文本进度追踪，无需修改原有 TTS 模型，具备良好的实用性和扩展性，为实时语音交互系统提供了关键支持。

Abstract: Incremental-text streaming text-to-speech (TTS) needs online text progress tracking for synchronized highlighting, interruption handling, and dialogue-history updates. Input text arrives before it is spoken, so text arrival alone cannot indicate speech progress. Existing waveform-based alignment requires complete audio or adds acoustic processing during streaming. We propose X2-NativeCursor, a lightweight observer that tracks progress from native speech tokens before waveform decoding without changing the TTS generator. Its normalization plan links spoken labels to their original-text spans. Text and native-token encoders feed a local matcher that estimates the current label position. A separate output rule converts revisable position estimates into a cursor that never moves backward. Mean absolute error against an automatic reference is 0.151 Chinese characters with 80-ms lookahead, versus 1.253 characters with 320-ms lookahead for an online waveform baseline. Alignment real-time factor also decreases from 0.3598 to 0.0180 relative to this baseline. Lower tracking error is retained under a second automatic alignment reference. We evaluate X2-NativeCursor on Qwen3-TTS and validate its adaptation to CosyVoice2 by training a separate observer for each backbone. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/X-Square-Robot/X2Streaming-TTS.

</details>


### [97] [Which Medical Questions Deserve Rationales? Perturbation-Sensitive Selection for Robust QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09684)
*Yuexin Wu,Dayou Yu,Vasile Rus*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究在固定预算下，如何选择已标注问题进行推理链监督，以提升医学问答模型的鲁棒性。提出RMS-RSP方法，通过扰动推理链中的隐藏状态来衡量黄金答案与干扰项之间的差距变化，从而优先选择更具判别力的样本。实验表明，尽管在平均准确率上仅略优于随机选择（60.61% vs 60.08%），但在答案选项重排后，其鲁棒性和语义一致性显著提升（分别+1.91和+2.85点），且在所有数据集上表现一致。全量标注推理链虽能提升宏观准确率至63.74%，但代价高昂且不保证鲁棒性改善。结论表明，基于局部边界敏感性的选择策略有助于增强模型对语义等价格式变化的不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学问答数据集中答案标签丰富，但高质量推理链稀缺、噪声大或验证成本高。因此，核心问题从‘应标注哪些问题’转变为‘在有限预算下，应为哪些已有标注的问题分配推理链监督’。目标是高效利用有限资源，提升模型对格式变化的鲁棒性与语义一致性。

Method: 提出根均方鲁棒性样本优先选择（RMS-RSP）方法：仅对推理链中的token进行隐藏状态扰动，测量其对黄金答案与最优干扰项之间得分差的影响，据此评估样本的重要性并排序。该方法在离线设置中运行，候选推理链可见但仅被选中者参与训练。

Result: 在五个医学QA数据集上，RMS-RSP在锁定预算下的平均准确率为60.61%，略高于随机选择的60.08%；仅在AfriMed-QA上实现统计显著提升（+1.44点）。全预算下未超越随机选择。然而，在三次答案选项重排后，其鲁棒性（+1.91点）和语义一致性（+2.85点）均显著提升，且方向一致于全部数据集。全量标注推理链可使宏准确率达63.74%，但需29–254倍更多推理令牌，且未统一提升鲁棒性。

Conclusion: RMS-RSP并未带来普遍的准确率提升，但证明了基于推理链局部边界敏感性的样本选择策略，能够有效识别出有助于提升模型对语义等价格式变化不变性的监督信号。这为有限预算下高质量推理链的高效部署提供了新思路。

Abstract: Medical question-answering datasets often contain answer labels, whereas high-quality rationales remain scarce, noisy, or costly to validate. This changes the acquisition question: rather than asking which questions should be labeled, we ask which already-labeled questions should receive rationale supervision under a fixed token budget. We study an offline version of this problem in which candidate rationales are visible to the selector but withheld from downstream training unless selected. We propose root-mean-square Robustness-based Sample Prioritization (RMS-RSP), which perturbs hidden states only at rationale tokens and measures the resulting shift in the gold-versus-best-distractor margin. Across five medical QA datasets, MedGemma-4B-IT, three training seeds, ten budgeted non-RSP selectors, and an unbudgeted full-supervision reference, RMS-RSP provides a deliberately qualified result. Its locked-budget accuracy is 60.61% on average versus 60.08% for Random, with a statistically resolved gain only on AfriMed-QA (+1.44 points). Its full-budget accuracy area is not better than Random. However, after three answer-option reorderings, RMS-RSP improves robust accuracy and semantic consistency by 1.91 and 2.85 points on average, respectively, with the same direction on all five datasets. Training on every pool rationale raises macro accuracy to 63.74%, but consumes 29--254 times more rationale tokens and does not uniformly improve robustness. These findings do not establish universal accuracy gains; they instead suggest that rationale-local boundary sensitivity can identify supervision that improves invariance to semantically equivalent formatting changes.

</details>


### [98] [Looped GPT-BERT: Trading Parameters for Computation in Small Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09691)
*Tingshuo Fan,Hongtao Mu,Tianyu Zhou,Hansen Liu,Tao Ji*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在数据有限的情况下，通过参数共享和循环机制，小规模模型也能达到与大模型相当的性能。本文研究了在BabyLM 2026 Strict-small设置下，结合GPT-BERT目标函数与深度参数共享的Looped GPT-BERT模型，使用748万词英语语料训练，最终模型为4×12结构（4个物理层，12次循环），仅含1218万参数，但在多个语言学和下游任务（如BLiMP、GLUE）上表现接近甚至优于更大模型，且在整体平均分（35.42）和NLP平均分（48.48）上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当训练数据有限时，单纯增加参数量并非提升语言模型性能的唯一途径。探索如何通过高效利用少量参数实现高性能，是提升模型效率的关键方向。本文旨在验证参数共享与循环计算能否在不增加参数量的前提下提升模型表现。

Method: 采用深度参数共享机制，在多层网络中引入循环遍历（looped architecture），将物理层数与循环次数分离，构建4×12结构的Looped GPT-BERT模型；结合掩码下一个词与因果语言建模双目标函数，在预处理后的748万词英文语料上进行训练，并系统比较不同目标比例、是否循环、循环次数等配置的影响。

Result: 所提模型在保持极低参数量（1218万）的同时，于BabyLM 2026排行榜上获得35.42的整体平均分和48.48的NLP平均分，优于或可比公共基准（如GPT-2、GPT-BERT），尤其在语言学任务（BLiMP）和下游任务（GLUE）上表现良好。循环机制有助于提升训练效果并维持语言任务性能，但受限于物理层数少，其表示能力存在瓶颈，导致部分任务表现不佳。

Conclusion: 在参数受限条件下，通过循环计算与参数共享，小模型仍可实现与大模型相近的性能。然而，由于物理层数限制，循环设计在表达能力方面存在固有局限，未来需进一步优化结构以突破该瓶颈。

Abstract: When training data are limited, increasing parameter count is not the only way to improve language-model performance. A small parameter set, when repeatedly applied, can also deliver comparable performance. We study Looped GPT-BERT in the BabyLM 2026 Strict-small setting, combining GPT-BERT's masked next-token and causal language-modeling objectives with depth-wise parameter sharing. We train on a preprocessed 7.48M-word English corpus and compare objective ratios, non-looped and looped architectures, and loop counts. Our final $4\times12$ model uses four physical layers for twelve recurrent traversals and contains 12.18M parameters. The BabyLM 2026 leaderboard reports an Overall Average of 35.42 and an NLP Average of 48.48. Compared with public BabyLM 10M Strict-small GPT-2 and GPT-BERT baselines, it achieves comparable performance on selected linguistic and downstream metrics, including BLiMP and GLUE, with fewer parameters. The loop ablations show that additional recurrent computation can improve training and preserve strong performance on selected linguistic tasks, whereas poorer performance on other tasks may reveal an inherent limitation of the looped design: using only a few physical layers restricts the model's representational space.

</details>


### [99] [When Auditors Fabricate: Batch-Size Degradation and Confident Hallucination in LLM Detection of Planted Document Contamination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09696)
*Karan Parekh,Sanjana Pendyala Ravinder,Sana Mhapsekar,Medina Maloku*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 大型语言模型在文档质量审计中的可靠性尚未得到充分验证。研究构建了一个包含150篇学术论文的污染语料库，注入450个已知错误（类型包括拼写错误、语义反转和不合逻辑的内容插入），并测试Google Gemini 3.0 Pro在三种不同提示规模下的检测能力。结果发现：小规模下检测有效（单文档50%，小批量60%），但在大规模时急剧下降至2.8%。失败模式并非回避，而是生成虚假发现，如‘心灵感应松鼠’‘量子烤面包机’等虚构错误。不同类型错误的检出率差异显著：荒谬插入物检出率75%，而语义反转和拼写错误仅为50%。现实中更可能发生的合理错误反而最易被遗漏。结论是LLM文档审计并非渐进退化，而是以欺骗性方式崩溃，必须采取限制批处理大小、直接注入内容、对每项报告结果进行机械验证等措施加以控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估大型语言模型作为自动文档质量审计工具的可靠性，特别是其在检测人为植入错误方面的表现，揭示其在实际应用中的潜在风险与局限性。

Method: 构建包含150篇跨领域学术论文的污染语料库，注入三类共450个已知错误；在三种不同提示规模（单文档、小批量、大批量）下测试Google Gemini 3.0 Pro的检测性能，并分析其错误识别率及失败模式。

Result: 小规模下检测准确率尚可（单文档50%，小批量60%），但大规模下骤降至2.8%；模型非因无法处理而放弃，而是主动编造错误，生成如‘心灵感应松鼠’等虚构问题；荒谬插入物检出率最高（75%），而现实常见且合理的语义反转和拼写错误检出率仅50%。

Conclusion: LLM文档审计系统在规模扩大时并非平稳退化，而是以制造虚假发现的方式出现欺骗性失效，必须通过控制批处理规模、强制内容注入、对每个报告结果进行机械验证来确保可信性。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly proposed as automated auditors of document quality, yet their reliability as detectors of planted errors is poorly characterised. We construct a contaminated corpus of 150 academic papers spanning supply chain management and medical research, injecting 450 known contaminants of three types: typographical corruption, semantic reversal, and absurd out-of-context insertion. We then evaluate Google Gemini 3.0 Pro's ability to recover a 180-contaminant answer-key subset across 60 documents under three prompting regimes of increasing scale: single document, small batch, and large batch. Detection holds at small scale and then collapses: 50% recovery on single documents, 60% on small batches, and 2.8% on large batches. The failure mode at scale is not abstention but fabrication. Rather than reporting incomplete processing, the model produced confident findings including invented contaminants of its own, absurdities such as "telepathic squirrel" and "quantum-powered toaster" that mimic the style of the planted material but do not appear in any document. Detection also varies by contamination type: absurd insertions were recovered at 75% in completed evaluations, while semantic reversals and typographical corruptions were each recovered at only 50%. The corruptions most likely to occur in the wild, plausible ones, are the ones most often missed. We conclude that LLM document auditing degrades not gracefully but deceptively, and outline the harness such systems require: bounded batch sizes, direct content injection, and mechanical verification of every reported finding against source text.

</details>


### [100] [Scaling E-Commerce Attribute Extraction with Parallel Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09716)
*Nikhita Vedula,Dushyanta Dhyani,Bryan Wang,Shervin Malmasi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种两阶段LLM流水线，先发现每个产品类别的购买差异化属性的紧凑排序模式，再用微调的Qwen3-4B模型结合超并行解码（HPD）提取属性值。该方法在保持85%提取准确率的同时，将推理成本降低92%，支持大规模生产应用，构建了结构化的自动产品知识库，可用于产品发现与目录增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商目录混乱且无结构，现有属性值提取系统对所有属性一视同仁，导致属性集庞大且不一致，无法反映消费者实际关注的差异因素。需要一种能识别关键购买属性并高效提取的方法。

Method: 采用两阶段LLM流水线：第一阶段通过大模型发现每个品类中最具购买区分度的紧凑、排序属性集合；第二阶段使用微调的Qwen3-4B模型结合超并行解码（HPD）从文本中高效提取属性值。

Result: 实现85%的属性提取准确率，与基础大模型相当，但推理成本降低92%，可支持生产级应用；生成的结构化属性表示可作为跨品类统一的产品知识库，支撑下游知识密集型任务。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效解决了电商目录中属性提取不精准、成本高的问题，构建了高质量、可扩展的自动产品知识库，为产品发现和智能推荐等应用提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: Customers rely on specific product attributes to compare products and make purchasing decisions, but e-commerce catalogs are messy and unstructured, making it difficult to identify which attributes matter most and extract them at scale. Standard Attribute Value Extraction (AVE) systems treat all attributes equally, producing large, inconsistent attribute sets that do not reflect the factors consumers use to differentiate products.
